<!-- page 1 -->
NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS’ MINUTES 
Tuesday, December 9, 2014 
 
PRESENT: Supervisor; Dawnafe Whitesinger, Supervisor; David Tenney, Supervisor; and Jesse Thompson, 
Chairman were present. Jason E. Whiting, Supervisor and Jonathan M. Nez, Supervisor were present 
telephonically. 
9:02 a.m.  Call To Order: Pledge of Allegiance and Invocation led by Supervisor Whitesinger.   
CALL TO THE PUBLIC: There were no public comments. 
      CONSENT AGENDA : a) Constable  Report: Pinetop -Lakeside Precinct #6, October 2014  b) Warrant & 
Voucher Report over $1000, November 2014 : Supervisor Tenney made a motion  to authorize the Chairman 
to sign the items in the consent agenda as presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote 
unanimous approving the motion.  
HUMAN RESOURCES:  
a. Retirement awards presented to Dara Davis (Sheriff’s Office) for her dedication to Navajo County for 
20+ years, Susan Clark for her dedication to Navajo County for 13+ years, and Judge Alison Kolomitz 
for her dedication to Navajo County for 15+ years: Kimberly Eavenson made the presentation. 
b. Consideration and possib le approval of Personnel Actions : Kimberly Eavenson reviewed the 26 items 
on the personnel action report. Supervisor Tenney made a motion  to approve the personnel actions as 
presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
EMERGENCY MANAGEMENT:  
a. Consideration and possible adoption of Resolution Number 35-14, approving the application to FEMA 
for access to IPAWS Integrated Public Alert & Warning System : Mary Springer discussed the item. 
James Jayne, County manager recognized Mrs. Springer and her team for their continued work with 
Emergency Management.  Supervisor Tenney made a motion  to adopt Resolution Number 35 -14, 
approving the application to FEMA f or access to IPAWS Integrated Public Alert & Warning System ; 
motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
b. Consideration and possible approval of the revised Navajo County Emergency Operations Plan : Mary 
Springer discussed the item. Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion  to approve the revised Navajo 
County Emergency Operations Plan ; motion seconded by Supervisor Tenney . Vote unanimous 
approving the motion. 
      ADMINISTRATION: Presentation on the AZ Town Hall on Economic Development: Hunter Moore, 
Governmental Affairs Director gave a presentation of the AZ town hall he attended on behalf of Navajo County.  
He indicated that the focus of the town hall  was economic development. Several topics were discussed during 
the sessions.  Education being one of the top discussions and how we fund education with the States budgets 
constraints.  Transportation and Public L ands was discussed pertaining to Forest and water issues.  The major 
topic for next ye ar is going to be about water. Hunter discussed issues of tribal government and smaller 
population areas. Mr. Moore  expressed how our C ounty Manager, James Jayne, is well known throughout the 
attendees and his leadership is respected . Chairman Thompson asked if Mr. Moore will present the 
PowerPoint in the coming future.  Mr. Moore stated he would share his presentation with the board. Supervisor 
Tenney asked if once you are invited you will be invited again.  County Manager, James Jayne commented that

<!-- page 2 -->
Board of Supervisor Minutes 12-09-2014 
    Page 2 of 3 
 
 
the conferences are topic specific so Mr. Vela has attended on the topics of transportation.  James Jayne sits on 
the AZ Town Hall board.   
COUNTY BUSINESS UPDATE: Report from Board members, County Attorney and County Manager: 
 Supervisor Nez, stated Merry Christmas to all have safe holidays. Supervisor Whiting was dropped on the call 
and was not able to give an update for District III. Supervisor Whitesinger stated that she serves on the board 
for the Safe House and they are having a couple of fund raisers.  The safe house is a domestic vi olence shelter 
in the Pinetop -Lakeside area and serve neighboring regions including the White Mountain Apache area, 
Holbrook and into Apache County .  We will be raising money by gift wrapping on Dec 14th & 15th and 20th & 
21st.  Supervisor Whitesinger will be there on the 20 th so if you would like her to wrap your gifts  at mountain 
high, please stop by .  She mentioned that the Christmas tree lot over by the tractor supply in Show Low has 
trees decorated by different organizations.  The White Mountain S afe House has a tree represented so you can 
stop by and donate for your particular  organization that you support. Supervisor Tenney  offered his 
congratulations to County Attorney, Brad Carlyon, on becoming AACo president  and commended him on the 
work he wi ll be doing there .  Supervisor Tenney  discussed an upcoming meeting with Congresswomen 
Kirkpatrick, Senator Flake and Department of Transportation in Washington, D .C. talking about financing for 
the Apache Railroad.  Another meeting schedule d this evening will be with the Region III Regional Forester, 
Cal Joyner, discussing a stewardship agreement.  Look forward to partnering with other to work on issues of 
wildfires and resto ring our forests.  Supervisor Tenney  also wished everyone a very Merry Christmas.  Mr. 
Jayne spoke about the budget book that was handed ou t and congratulated Mr. Menlove and his team for the 
work they provided and reported that Friday the (CAFR,) Comprehensive Annual Financial R eport would be 
out from the Auditor General and Navajo County is first for the 2nd year in a row.  Staff applauded Mr. Menlove 
for his leadership. Mr. Jayne confirmed that Governor Brewer appointed Dale Nielson as Superior Court Judge, 
effective January 1, 2015.  Mr. Jayne commented that the completion date for the new jail is scheduled for the 
end of January  and the  open house will be scheduled on the first board meeting in February.  On Tuesday, 
December 30, 2014 in the board chambers we will host Swearing-In ceremony for the newly elect ed and re -
elected officials.  He reminded those of the open house for the new Public Works building today at 1:30pm.  
Mr. Jayne mentioned the 2014 Summit award that Navajo County won at this year’s AACo conference.  County 
Attorney Brad Carlyon congratulat ed Supervisor Tenney on being selected for the Governor Elect Transition 
Team.  Supervisor Tenney wanted to thank Mr. Menlove and his team for getting the county budgets in line.  
When he arrived the County was 5 years behind on budgets.  Chairman Thompson congratulated the County 
Attorney for becoming the AACo President.  Chairman Thompson talked about the Navajo County Christmas 
Party and about what a good time it was.  Thanks to staff and Supervisor Tenney for the holiday games and 
good laughs. Supervisor Tenney  congratulated Sheriff Clark for his appointment as Vice President of the 
Arizona County Attorney’s Sheriff’s Association.    
ADJOURN: At 9:41  a.m. Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion to adjourn ; motion seconded by 
Supervisor Tenney. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
APPROVED:       DATED: January 13, 2015 
 
David Tenney, Chairman 
Navajo County Board of Supervisors
